Their scattering at 82:2 resonates with an ordered sky whose markers can also be displaced.","ayah_refs":["82:2","82:7","82:6"]},{"parent_channel":"Sky, Height, and Atmospheric Display","parent_semantic_invariant":"Things become perceptible through elevation, overhead layering, celestial light, or weather-borne display.","parent_surprising_reach":"The sky scene extends into layered cloud, nocturnal moisture, calendrical visibility, public reputation, and animals used as celestial analogues.","key":"C","name":"Night Moisture, Southern Breeze, and Fertile Rain","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Moisture arrives at night or on a mild southern wind and supports fertile land.","active_motifs":"nighttime droplets on grass `ك و ك ب:B004/m01`; mild moist south wind `ن ع م:B009/m01`; rain-bearing generous cloud `ك ر م:B002/m01`; sky as rain source `س م و:B004/m02`.","ayah_anchors":"ك و ك ب at 82:2; ن ع م at 82:13; ك ر م at 82:6/11; س م و at 82:1.","synthesis":"Dew, soft wind, and rain form one atmospheric delivery process. The channel connects the elevated sky to the moisture by which terrestrial fertility becomes visible.","ayah_refs":["82:2","82:13","82:6","82:1"]},{"parent_channel":"Sky, Height, and Atmospheric Display","parent_semantic_invariant":"Things become perceptible through elevation, overhead layering, celestial light, or weather-borne display.","parent_surprising_reach":"The sky scene extends into layered cloud, nocturnal moisture, calendrical visibility, public reputation, and animals used as celestial analogues.","key":"D","name":"Bright Prominence and Public Renown","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A person or place stands out as a bright, elevated, or widely noticed presence.","active_motifs":"good renown carried abroad `س م و:B008/m01`; brightness of a meadow `ك و ك ب:B006/m01`; conspicuous whiteness `غ ر ر:B003/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"س م و at 82:1; ك و ك ب at 82:2; غ ر ر at 82:6.","synthesis":"Physical visibility and social visibility share the invariant of standing out from a field. A bright patch, luminous meadow, and good reputation all become forms of perceptible prominence.","ayah_refs":["82:1","82:2","82:6"]},{"parent_channel":"Rupture, Dispersal, and Exposure","parent_semantic_invariant":"A closed, ordered, or concealed arrangement is opened, scattered, overturned, or made visible.","parent_surprising_reach":"The same mechanics reach folded material, worn cloth, confused crowds, migrating groups, and overturned basins.","key":"E","name":"Crease, Wear, and Concealed Material Damage","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Folding leaves a trace, and repeated use turns that trace into wear or a hidden defect.","active_motifs":"visible crease left by a fold `غ ر ر:B001/m01`; folding while damp and carrying a flaw `ب ل ل:B007/m01`; cloth worn smooth, frayed, or threadbare `خ ل ق:B009/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"غ ر ر at 82:6; ب ل ل at 82:9; خ ل ق at 82:7.","synthesis":"The crease is the first material disturbance, damp folding preserves it, and wear is its durable outcome. The channel is a small-scale material analogue of larger rupture and exposure.","ayah_refs":["82:6","82:9","82:7"]},{"parent_channel":"Water, Moisture, and Sustenance","parent_semantic_invariant":"Water gathers, spreads, moistens, fills, or sustains life while its absence produces thirst.","parent_surprising_reach":"Water appears not only as sea and pool but as a travel resource, a measured draught, a filled vessel, and a determinant of settlement.","key":"A","name":"Broad and Saline Waters","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A large body of water spreads across an open expanse and may become strongly saline.","active_motifs":"abundant broad water `ب ح ر:B001/m01`; salinity or brine `ب ح ر:B004/m01`; deep gathered water `ع ل م:B005/m01`; abundant collected water `ر ب ب:B013/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"ب ح ر at 82:3; ع ل م at 82:5/12; ر ب ب at 82:6.","synthesis":"Breadth, depth, gathering, and salinity describe the physical state of a sea or deep well. They amplify the surface plural seas into a full hydrological body.","ayah_refs":["82:3","82:5","82:6"]},{"parent_channel":"Water, Moisture, and Sustenance","parent_semantic_invariant":"Water gathers, spreads, moistens, fills, or sustains life while its absence produces thirst.","parent_surprising_reach":"Water appears not only as sea and pool but as a travel resource, a measured draught, a filled vessel, and a determinant of settlement.","key":"D","name":"Filling, Wetting, and Pouring","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Liquid is driven into a vessel or surface until it becomes full or wet.","active_motifs":"filling a waterskin or feeding liquid inward `غ ر ر:B011/m01`; wetness and remaining moisture `ب ل ل:B001/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"غ ر ر at 82:6; ب ل ل at 82:9.","synthesis":"The vessel supplies containment, filling supplies movement, and wetness supplies the resulting state. The process bridges water flow with storage.","ayah_refs":["82:6","82:9"]},{"parent_channel":"Creation, Form, and Development","parent_semantic_invariant":"An entity is originated, measured, assembled, proportioned, and brought toward a mature form.","parent_surprising_reach":"The formation sequence extends into planning, fitted components, premature material, emerging teeth, smooth surfaces, and blocked forms.","key":"E","name":"Nurture, Maturation, and Premature Emergence","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A living or worked thing develops by stages but can emerge before its process is complete.","active_motifs":"gradual nurture and completion `ر ب ب:B002/m01`; attainment of mature strength `س و ي:B005/m01`; dough or material used before ripening `ف ط ر:B006/m01`; tooth breaking through `ف ط ر:B007/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"ر ب ب at 82:6; س و ي and خ ل ق at 82:7; ف ط ر at 82:1.","synthesis":"Nurture describes sustained development, maturity its expected end, and premature use or tooth eruption the moment of incomplete or forceful emergence. The scene extends creation into temporal development.","ayah_refs":["82:6","82:7","82:1"]},{"parent_channel":"Knowledge, Perception, Signs, and Records","parent_semantic_invariant":"Information becomes available through perception, learning, memory, marking, or durable inscription.","parent_surprising_reach":"Knowledge is embodied in hearing, memory, road signs, templates, banners, registers, and the tension between hiddenness and doubt.","key":"A","name":"Knowing, Learning, and Sage Formation","reading_type":"surface-primary","scene_or_process":"A knower apprehends information, develops expertise, and can transmit it.","active_motifs":"disclosed knowledge `ع ل م:B001/m01`; practical knowing and informing `د ر ي:B001/m01`; cultivated learned person `ر ب ب:B003/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"ع ل م at 82:5/12; د ر ي at 82:17/18; ر ب ب at 82:6.","synthesis":"Apprehension supplies the cognitive event, practical knowledge supplies mastery, and cultivated learning supplies the formed knower. Together they expand the repeated epistemic challenge of the surah.","ayah_refs":["82:5","82:17","82:6"]},{"parent_channel":"Knowledge, Perception, Signs, and Records","parent_semantic_invariant":"Information becomes available through perception, learning, memory, marking, or durable inscription.","parent_surprising_reach":"Knowledge is embodied in hearing, memory, road signs, templates, banners, registers, and the tension between hiddenness and doubt.","key":"B","name":"Detection, Hearing, and Farsight","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A perceiver detects an object through sight, sound, sensation, or long-range observation.","active_motifs":"seeing, hearing, or sensing a presence `ء ن س:B002/m01`; deliberate listening `ش ي ء:B005/m01`; long sight `ش ي ء:B003/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"ء ن س at 82:6; ش ي ء at 82:8/19.","synthesis":"Detection names the general perceptual act, listening its auditory mode, and farsight its extended visual mode. The channel preserves perception as a route into knowledge rather than conflating it with learned cognition.","ayah_refs":["82:6","82:8"]},{"parent_channel":"Knowledge, Perception, Signs, and Records","parent_semantic_invariant":"Information becomes available through perception, learning, memory, marking, or durable inscription.","parent_surprising_reach":"Knowledge is embodied in hearing, memory, road signs, templates, banners, registers, and the tension between hiddenness and doubt.","key":"D","name":"Marks, Banners, Landmarks, and Templates","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A visible sign distinguishes an object, guides a traveler, or provides a model for repeated work.","active_motifs":"distinguishing mark, banner, or landmark `ع ل م:B002/m01`; road sign or appointed marker `ء م ر:B005/m01`; model and single guiding course `غ ر ر:B002/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"ع ل م at 82:5/12; ء م ر at 82:19; غ ر ر at 82:6.","synthesis":"A mark identifies, a landmark or appointment orients, and a template governs repetition. The scene unifies semiotic and practical guidance through a visible differentiator.","ayah_refs":["82:5","82:19","82:6"]},{"parent_channel":"Judgment, Balance, and Deferred Exchange","parent_semantic_invariant":"Claims, persons, or obligations are assessed through judgment, equivalence, counterweight, deliberation, or settlement.","parent_surprising_reach":"Judicial balance extends into ransom, balanced loads, credit risk, debt records, and delegated credibility.","key":"E","name":"Debt, Credit, and Deferred Settlement","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A transfer occurs now while payment, release, or completion remains due later.","active_motifs":"financial debt and credit `د ي ن:B003/m01`; sale or payment deferred to a later time `ء خ ر:B002/m01`; uncertain commercial outcome `غ ر ر:B009/m01`; emancipation contract paid by installments `ك ت ب:B005/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"د ي ن at 82:9/15/17/18; ء خ ر at 82:5; غ ر ر at 82:6; ك ت ب at 82:11.","synthesis":"Debt establishes obligation, deferment separates exchange from settlement, risk clouds the outcome, and the installment contract turns payment into release. The scene is coherent commercial temporality rather than generic lateness.","ayah_refs":["82:9","82:5","82:6","82:11"]},{"parent_channel":"Sovereignty, Possession, and Polity","parent_semantic_invariant":"Affairs are ordered through command, ownership, rule, institutional settlement, and recognized leadership.","parent_surprising_reach":"Sovereignty reaches city organization, practical mainstays, animal leaders, maritime command, and the vulnerable follower who depends on others' decisions.","key":"A","name":"Lordship, Kingship, and Command","reading_type":"surface-primary","scene_or_process":"A sovereign possesses recognized authority over subjects and affairs.","active_motifs":"kingship and political sovereignty `م ل ك:B003/m01`; lordship, mastery, and ownership `ر ب ب:B001/m01`; office of rule `ء م ر:B003/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"م ل ك and ء م ر at 82:19; ر ب ب at 82:6.","synthesis":"Lordship supplies the master-subject relation, kingship supplies public sovereignty, and office supplies institutional form. The convergence sharpens the transfer of all command to God in 82:19.","ayah_refs":["82:19","82:6"]},{"parent_channel":"Sovereignty, Possession, and Polity","parent_semantic_invariant":"Affairs are ordered through command, ownership, rule, institutional settlement, and recognized leadership.","parent_surprising_reach":"Sovereignty reaches city organization, practical mainstays, animal leaders, maritime command, and the vulnerable follower who depends on others' decisions.","key":"D","name":"Vanguard, Animal Guide, and Shipmaster","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A leading figure moves first and directs a following body.","active_motifs":"animal leader followed by the herd `م ل ك:B008/m01`; forepart and vanguard `ق د م:B007/m01`; chief of sailors `ر ب ب:B017/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"م ل ك at 82:19; ق د م at 82:5; ر ب ب at 82:6.","synthesis":"Vanguard gives spatial precedence, the herd leader gives follower structure, and the shipmaster gives skilled command. They are concrete analogues of authority as directional leadership.","ayah_refs":["82:19","82:5","82:6"]},{"parent_channel":"Worship, Observance, and Covenant","parent_semantic_invariant":"A subject binds conduct to a sacred authority through worship, prayer, repeated observance, or protected covenant.","parent_surprising_reach":"The sacred frame includes houses of worship, breaking a fast, protective honor, treaty, and sacrilege.","key":"B","name":"Supplication, Blessing, and Sacred Invocation","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A speaker invokes God or asks blessing and mercy for another.","active_motifs":"prayer, blessing, and mercy `ص ل ي:B002/m01`; divine name in invocation `ء ل ه:B002/m01`; gracious prayer-response formula `ك ر م:B009/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"ص ل ي at 82:15; ء ل ه at 82:19; ك ر م at 82:6/11.","synthesis":"Invocation names the addressed authority, supplication performs the request, and the gracious response completes the exchange. The channel joins sacred speech with beneficent social address.","ayah_refs":["82:15","82:19","82:6"]},{"parent_channel":"Worship, Observance, and Covenant","parent_semantic_invariant":"A subject binds conduct to a sacred authority through worship, prayer, repeated observance, or protected covenant.","parent_surprising_reach":"The sacred frame includes houses of worship, breaking a fast, protective honor, treaty, and sacrilege.","key":"D","name":"Covenant, Protected Honor, and Sacrilege","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A binding pact protects persons and sacred limits until violation turns the relation into sacrilege.","active_motifs":"treaty and protected compact `ر ب ب:B011/m01`; safeguarding covenant and inviolable honor `ح ف ظ:B006/m01`; violation of sacred limits in the Days of Fijar `ف ج ر:B006/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"ر ب ب at 82:6; ح ف ظ at 82:10; ف ج ر at 82:3/14.","synthesis":"Covenant establishes obligation, protection maintains it, and sacrilege names its rupture. The scene preserves fidelity and violation as opposite outcomes of one sacred social frame.","ayah_refs":["82:6","82:10","82:3"]},{"parent_channel":"Virtue, Benefaction, and Honor","parent_semantic_invariant":"Good character becomes socially effective through truth, generosity, aid, praise, dignity, and gracious response.","parent_surprising_reach":"Moral excellence extends into abundant flow, social welfare, prior merit, reputation, gift exchange, and verbal courtesy.","key":"B","name":"Generosity as Abundant Increase","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Benefit flows beyond sufficiency and enlarges the recipient's condition.","active_motifs":"generosity pouring forth widely `ف ج ر:B005/m01`; noble generosity `ك ر م:B001/m01`; increase beyond the initial act `ن ع م:B010/m01`; growth and blessing `ء م ر:B004/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"ف ج ر at 82:3/14; ك ر م at 82:6/11; ن ع م at 82:13; ء م ر at 82:19.","synthesis":"Flow supplies the image, nobility the agent quality, increase the operation, and blessing the outcome. The cluster makes generosity a productive process rather than a static trait.","ayah_refs":["82:3","82:6","82:13","82:19"]},{"parent_channel":"Virtue, Benefaction, and Honor","parent_semantic_invariant":"Good character becomes socially effective through truth, generosity, aid, praise, dignity, and gracious response.","parent_surprising_reach":"Moral excellence extends into abundant flow, social welfare, prior merit, reputation, gift exchange, and verbal courtesy.","key":"C","name":"Welfare, Aid, and Favor","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A need is met by benefit, support, or a favor that restores good condition.","active_motifs":"material benefit and social support `ب ل ل:B002/m01`; need, favor, or tightly held benefit `ر ب ب:B016/m01`; good condition and bestowed ease `ن ع م:B001/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"ب ل ل at 82:9; ر ب ب at 82:6; ن ع م at 82:13.","synthesis":"Need supplies the deficit, support supplies intervention, and ease supplies the resulting condition. This is benefaction viewed from the recipient's welfare.","ayah_refs":["82:9","82:6","82:13"]},{"parent_channel":"Virtue, Benefaction, and Honor","parent_semantic_invariant":"Good character becomes socially effective through truth, generosity, aid, praise, dignity, and gracious response.","parent_surprising_reach":"Moral excellence extends into abundant flow, social welfare, prior merit, reputation, gift exchange, and verbal courtesy.","key":"D","name":"Honor, Prior Merit, and Elevated Standing","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A person receives standing because of noble quality or an established record of merit.","active_motifs":"honor and noble excellence `ك ر م:B001/m02`; prior merit or distinguished precedence `ق د م:B002/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"ك ر م at 82:6/11; ق د م at 82:5.","synthesis":"Noble quality supplies intrinsic worth and prior merit supplies an established history. Together they distinguish earned standing from mere display.","ayah_refs":["82:6","82:5"]},{"parent_channel":"Virtue, Benefaction, and Honor","parent_semantic_invariant":"Good character becomes socially effective through truth, generosity, aid, praise, dignity, and gracious response.","parent_surprising_reach":"Moral excellence extends into abundant flow, social welfare, prior merit, reputation, gift exchange, and verbal courtesy.","key":"E","name":"Gift, Praise, and Courteous Response","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A gift or commendation invites acknowledgment, reward, or a gracious verbal reply.","active_motifs":"gift offered in expectation of recompense `ك ر م:B008/m01`; courteous answer and willing acceptance `ك ر م:B009/m02`; explicit praise or commendation `ن ع م:B003/m01`; prayer for comfort of the eye `ن ع م:B013/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"ك ر م at 82:6/11; ن ع م at 82:13.","synthesis":"Gift initiates exchange, praise evaluates it, courtesy answers it, and blessing extends goodwill to the recipient. The scene joins material and verbal forms of honor.","ayah_refs":["82:6","82:13"]},{"parent_channel":"Falsehood, Deception, and Breach","parent_semantic_invariant":"Appearance, speech, or conduct presents something other than the truth or fails a trusted relation.","parent_surprising_reach":"Deception includes fabricated language, misleading cloth and shine, naivety, hazardous exchange, concealed flaws, and a soul characterized by falsehood.","key":"B","name":"Misleading Appearance","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"An attractive or patterned surface claims a condition it does not actually possess.","active_motifs":"lure through transient glitter or ornament `غ ر ر:B008/m01`; patterned cloth that lies by its appearance `ك ذ ب:B009/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"غ ر ر at 82:6; ك ذ ب at 82:9.","synthesis":"Glitter supplies attraction and the patterned cloth supplies a concrete deceptive object. Both make visual appeal the vehicle of false representation.","ayah_refs":["82:6","82:9"]},{"parent_channel":"Falsehood, Deception, and Breach","parent_semantic_invariant":"Appearance, speech, or conduct presents something other than the truth or fails a trusted relation.","parent_surprising_reach":"Deception includes fabricated language, misleading cloth and shine, naivety, hazardous exchange, concealed flaws, and a soul characterized by falsehood.","key":"C","name":"Naivety, Bewilderment, and Hazard","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"An inexperienced or confused actor enters a situation whose outcome is uncertain.","active_motifs":"inexperience and easy credulity `غ ر ر:B007/m01`; hazard with unknown outcome `غ ر ر:B009/m02`; bewilderment or stupefaction `ب ح ر:B008/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"غ ر ر at 82:6; ب ح ر at 82:3.","synthesis":"Inexperience weakens judgment, bewilderment disturbs it, and hazard defines the uncertain environment. This is the cognitive mechanism by which a lure can succeed.","ayah_refs":["82:6","82:3"]},{"parent_channel":"Time, Sequence, and Decisive Event","parent_semantic_invariant":"Things are ordered by duration, beginning, precedence, delay, recurrence, or a singular event that changes the state of affairs.","parent_surprising_reach":"Temporal order includes dawn, ancient duration, delayed transactions, habitual practice, sudden multitudes, medical crisis, and arrival from travel.","key":"E","name":"Habit, Continuity, and Residence","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A condition persists because an actor repeats it or remains in place.","active_motifs":"customary state or habitual practice `د ي ن:B005/m01`; staying and enduring in a place `ر ب ب:B007/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"د ي ن at 82:9/15/17/18; ر ب ب at 82:6.","synthesis":"Habit provides repeated action and residence provides sustained presence. Their shared invariant is continuity across time.","ayah_refs":["82:9","82:6"]},{"parent_channel":"Orientation, Travel, and Pursuit","parent_semantic_invariant":"A participant adopts a direction, follows a route, moves by foot or mount, or pursues a target.","parent_surprising_reach":"Orientation extends into body-sidedness, road centers, sea travel, hunting concealment, accidental encounter, capture, and balanced riding gear.","key":"F","name":"Forepart, Hindpart, and Human-Facing Side","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"An object or body is oriented by front, rear, and the side turned toward its user.","active_motifs":"forepart or vanguard `ق د م:B007/m02`; rear or hind section `ء خ ر:B003/m01`; human-facing side of an object or mount `ء ن س:B004/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"ق د م and ء خ ر at 82:5; ء ن س at 82:6.","synthesis":"Front and rear define an axis, while the human-facing side situates a participant relative to it. The channel supplies concrete spatial anatomy to the surah's before-and-after language.","ayah_refs":["82:5","82:6"]},{"parent_channel":"Competition, Precedence, and Courage","parent_semantic_invariant":"Participants compare rank through racing, rivalry, boasting, daring, or decisive advance.","parent_surprising_reach":"Competition joins race order, social boasting, precious stakes, contentious character, battle advance, and an animal that runs then stops.","key":"B","name":"Rivalry, Boasting, and Social Victory","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Rivals publicly compare excellence and seek to surpass one another.","active_motifs":"mutual rivalry and boasting `س م و:B007/m01`; contest in generosity `ك ر م:B006/m01`; overcoming and precedence `ب ر ر:B008/m01`; severe contentiousness `ب ل ل:B005/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"س م و at 82:1; ك ر م at 82:6/11; ب ر ر at 82:13; ب ل ل at 82:9.","synthesis":"Rivalry establishes comparison, boasting publicizes it, contentiousness intensifies it, and victory supplies the outcome. The channel covers social competition beyond literal racing.","ayah_refs":["82:1","82:6","82:13","82:9"]},{"parent_channel":"Competition, Precedence, and Courage","parent_semantic_invariant":"Participants compare rank through racing, rivalry, boasting, daring, or decisive advance.","parent_surprising_reach":"Competition joins race order, social boasting, precious stakes, contentious character, battle advance, and an animal that runs then stops.","key":"C","name":"Precious Stakes and Competitive Desire","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A valued object becomes the focus of desire, guarded status, and competition.","active_motifs":"precious object over which people compete `ن ف س:B010/m01`; elite or finest member of a group `غ ر ر:B004/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"ن ف س at 82:5/19; غ ر ر at 82:6.","synthesis":"Value creates desire and elite standing concentrates it. The scene shows why prestige and rivalry cluster around scarce or honored objects.","ayah_refs":["82:5","82:6"]},{"parent_channel":"Craft, Materials, and Tools","parent_semantic_invariant":"Raw material is measured, joined, sharpened, fired, ground, contained, decorated, or worked by a skilled hand.","parent_surprising_reach":"The craft field includes stitched leather, spear assembly, iron sparkle, adzes, grinding stones, vessel lids, perfume, necklaces, and premature dough.","key":"B","name":"Edge, Horn, Spearhead, and Adze","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A hard implement receives a pointed or cutting end suited to combing, piercing, or carving.","active_motifs":"cutting edge of blade or sword `غ ر ر:B012/m01`; pointed comb-like implement `د ر ي:B004/m01`; adze used to carve wood `ق د م:B008/m01`; fitting a spear shaft with a point `ء م ر:B011/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"غ ر ر at 82:6; د ر ي at 82:17/18; ق د م at 82:5; ء م ر at 82:19.","synthesis":"Hard material supplies the component, sharpening supplies form, and fitting supplies assembly. The tool's function follows from the relation between shaft, point, and worked surface.","ayah_refs":["82:6","82:17","82:5","82:19"]},{"parent_channel":"Craft, Materials, and Tools","parent_semantic_invariant":"Raw material is measured, joined, sharpened, fired, ground, contained, decorated, or worked by a skilled hand.","parent_surprising_reach":"The craft field includes stitched leather, spear assembly, iron sparkle, adzes, grinding stones, vessel lids, perfume, necklaces, and premature dough.","key":"E","name":"Vessel, Lid, Bag, and Thick Paste","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A container is made useful by enclosure, covering, and a substance that coats or seals it.","active_motifs":"carried sack or bag `غ ر ر:B013/m01`; lid set over a jar or pot `ك ر م:B005/m01`; thick syrup or paste used in treatment `ر ب ب:B006/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"غ ر ر and ر ب ب at 82:6; ك ر م at 82:6/11.","synthesis":"The bag supplies portable containment, the lid closes an opening, and the paste conditions or seals the material. The scene unifies container components and maintenance.","ayah_refs":["82:6"]},{"parent_channel":"Craft, Materials, and Tools","parent_semantic_invariant":"Raw material is measured, joined, sharpened, fired, ground, contained, decorated, or worked by a skilled hand.","parent_surprising_reach":"The craft field includes stitched leather, spear assembly, iron sparkle, adzes, grinding stones, vessel lids, perfume, necklaces, and premature dough.","key":"F","name":"Necklace and Anointment","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A body or object is adorned through a strung ornament or scented coating.","active_motifs":"necklace or strung collar `ك ر م:B003/m01`; scented anointment or colored perfume `خ ل ق:B010/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"ك ر م at 82:6/11; خ ل ق at 82:7.","synthesis":"Stringing produces the ornament and anointment changes surface scent or color. Both are deliberate additions to appearance rather than changes to underlying form.","ayah_refs":["82:6","82:7"]},{"parent_channel":"Body, Physiology, and Health","parent_semantic_invariant":"Life is maintained and perceived through breath, blood, sensory organs, reproduction, nourishment, and bodily integrity.","parent_surprising_reach":"The body ledger reaches nostrils, pupils, blood depth, pelvic anatomy, lactation, wasting disease, crisis heat, paunch, scalp itch, and the birth caul.","key":"B","name":"Eye, Pupil, Gaze, and Envy","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"The eye receives an image and directs a charged gaze toward another.","active_motifs":"reflected human figure in the pupil `ء ن س:B005/m01`; inflamed or protruding eye `ج ح م:B003/m01`; envious or harmful eye `ن ف س:B003/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"ء ن س at 82:6; ج ح م at 82:14; ن ف س at 82:5/19.","synthesis":"The pupil supplies optical reception, the protruding eye supplies visible intensity, and the harmful gaze supplies social effect. The scene spans anatomy, perception, and interpersonal force.","ayah_refs":["82:6","82:14","82:5"]},{"parent_channel":"Body, Physiology, and Health","parent_semantic_invariant":"Life is maintained and perceived through breath, blood, sensory organs, reproduction, nourishment, and bodily integrity.","parent_surprising_reach":"The body ledger reaches nostrils, pupils, blood depth, pelvic anatomy, lactation, wasting disease, crisis heat, paunch, scalp itch, and the birth caul.","key":"D","name":"Knee, Pubis, Loins, and Hip Socket","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Lower-body joints and reproductive regions support movement, posture, and generation.","active_motifs":"knee and its bone `ر ك ب:B005/m01`; pubic or genital region `ر ك ب:B006/m01`; loins around the tailbone `ص ل ي:B006/m01`; rounded head of the thigh in the hip socket `ك ر م:B007/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"ر ك ب and ص و ر at 82:8; ص ل ي at 82:15; ك ر م at 82:6/11.","synthesis":"Knee supplies locomotor articulation, the hip supplies a rotating joint, and pubis plus loins locate reproductive anatomy. ## Your response

Write in tr. Produce your response as three parts, in this order. If you return one combined response, label the parts clearly. If an orchestrator asks you to write the parts into separate files, omit wrapper labels from the prose and evidence files; the file path already supplies the label.

1. **The prose** — per the output contract in `COMMENTARY_SPEC.md` §5 and the task document above: continuous, single voice, no provenance markers, no headers named after evidence layers.
2. **The evidence surface** — separate from the prose, addressable per phrase, per `PRINCIPLES.md` §12.
3. A section headed exactly `=== PROMPT FRICTION ===` reporting honestly where the specification above was unclear, self-contradictory, underspecified, or impossible to follow, and any point where you had to invent a rule to proceed. Report this section every time, even if the run went cleanly — say so explicitly if you found nothing. This is how the specification gets improved.

## V2 Rendering Profile — 5.6 Sol High

This run tests whether `5.6-sol-high` can increase lexical depth while preserving
reader-facing clarity.

Increase depth through semantic precision, not additional bulk.

Before drafting, silently build a coverage ledger:

- include every `must_integrate` topic;
- include every `candidate` with a reader payoff not already expressed;
- if `v12_reader_responses` is absent because the default workflow retired
  per-ayah focus runs, do not infer a `stage_00` isolated response or
  confidence movement;
- if `v12_focus_trace_hermetic` is present, use its `baseline_models`,
  `context_deltas`, and `surprising_valid_outliers` as reconstructed
  before/after evidence. Do not call it a staged reveal transcript. Preserve
  surprising outliers when they remain anchored in this ayah, especially
  secondary split-root activations;
- include a regular reader-walk, plus/minus-5 reader-walk, whole-surah reading,
  or cross-run-publication item only when it adds a distinct retrospective
  insight or coverage check;
- use branch inventories to support explanations; they create no standalone
  prose obligation. When fallback inventories are present, treat them as
  restricted to this ayah's roots and anchored citations, not as a whole-surah
  obligation.
- treat `root_lexicon` as full dictionary/gloss support, not branch-filtered.
  It uses QAC-to-Furuq root mapping; when a QAC root is split, non-dominant
  Furuq targets are included as additional root entries and recorded in
  coverage. If multiple QAC roots map to the same Furuq root, the shared entry
  records all source roots in `qac_roots_ar` / `qac_root_mappings`; use those
  fields for evidence attribution when present, not the legacy single `root_ar`
  alone. Dictionary/gloss branches are evidence support, not independent
  obligations, unless tied to this ayah's word, topic, reader payoff, or cited
  relation. Full field means all distinct reader payoffs from activated
  material, not every dictionary branch for the root.
- if `channel_generated_outputs` lists quran-data files and this run gives you
  file access, read only those listed files when channel-family/path detail is
  necessary. Treat them as candidate/family/path evidence, not as an adjudicated
  channel ledger. State B channel restrictions still apply.

For each critical word, preserve these distinct layers when available:

1. local grammatical work;
2. locally selected sense;
3. coherent pressure supplied by activated or cited root branches;
4. one form, sound, rarity, or variant observation with a unique payoff;
5. later contextual change, compressed to its final reader-visible result.

Do not flatten these layers into a general metaphor. Deepen generic summaries by
naming the exact lexical mechanism. Ordering grammar and local meaning first is
grounding, not truth-ranking.

At first mention of an ayah word, use a structured Arabic surface span:
`{ar:surface_form, tr:Turkish-readable transliteration, gloss:target-language meaning}`.
Use the same full span again when the prose returns to that word after moving to
another word or another paragraph. Inside one short local sequence, after a full
span has just been given, a Turkish label or transliteration is enough. Explain
every technical term in the same sentence. Keep raw roots, root skeletons,
letter-by-letter root transliterations, and branch IDs in the evidence surface.
In prose, attach root discussion to the surface word:
`{ar:ٱلْعَادِيَاتِ, tr:el-âdiyât, gloss:koşup atılanlar} kelimesinin bağlı
olduğu kök alanı...`, not `ʿ-d-v kökü...`.

A paragraph may begin with the ayah's surface, a concrete image, or a
reader-facing claim. It may not begin with a bare grammar label or stacked
abstractions. Prefer "Âyet önce hamdi Allah'a verir; sabit ad cümlesi bu hamdi
yerleşik bir hüküm olarak taşır" over "Bu âyet, tek bir isim cümlesiyle yerleşik
bir hüküm kurar."

For this short ayah, keep at least two-thirds of the prose on its own wording.
Compress all later developments into at most three paragraphs and end with one
plain synthesis paragraph.

Use `v12_cross_run_publication`, if present, only as a compact coverage/priority
check derived from regular and plus/minus-5 reader runs. Do not copy it as prose,
and do not let it override local bundle evidence.

Use an explicit negative predicate only to correct a likely misconception,
protect the primary sense from replacement, or preserve live counter-evidence.
Default ceiling for this ayah: three. Never stack two negatives in one sentence.
During final revision, rewrite all other negatives as positive predication.
Do not drive negation to zero by default: if no explicit negative is needed,
include a profile-specific style audit in friction confirming that there was no
live misconception, replacement risk, or counter-evidence requiring one.

Pass only if every paragraph has one distinct reader payoff and the prose remains
clear with the evidence surface closed.
